# Audio credits

All audio in this folder is either public domain (CC0 1.0) or licensed under Creative Commons Attribution 4.0.
Files were trimmed, converted to MP3 (44.1 kHz) and loudness-normalised for the game; the CC-BY 4.0 music is modified only in that way (boss/location tracks are also cut to a loopable excerpt with a short crossfade at the loop point).

CC0 sounds need no attribution; they are credited here as a courtesy.
Files credited as "Original synthesized sound" (and the synthesized layers noted on others) were made for Feetza with ffmpeg and Python (generators in `scripts/boss-sfx/`, `scripts/throw-sfx/`, `scripts/smack-sfx/`, `scripts/act8-sfx/`, `scripts/finale-audio/`, `scripts/ability-sfx/` and `scripts/pizza-sfx/`) and are released as CC0.
Some sounds are built from several Freesound recordings by `scripts/real-sfx/build.py` (trimmed, pitch-shifted and layered with ffmpeg, the Spartan, Skald and Siren sounds also given a short room, and the Act 8 ambience beds crossfaded into 50 s loops); each source has its own row above, and the CC-BY ones are credited with their attribution.
Kenney asset packs: https://kenney.nl (CC0). Freesound: https://freesound.org.
